# T6 — THE KITCHEN SCREEN · phases P02501–P03000

Territory (the only files this terminal may write to):

- `app/kitchen/layout.tsx`
- `app/kitchen/page.tsx`
- `public/panels/kitchen/index.html`
- `public/panels/kitchen/style.css`
- `public/panels/kitchen/app.js`

Everything else in the repo was read-only for this run. Where the real fix lived in someone
else's file the row is marked `🔗 HANDOFF`.

**Budget split (as briefed).**

| share | phases | what it is |
|---|---|---|
| ~40% | P02501–P02700 (200) | reading the code for correctness |
| ~20% | P02701–P02800 (100) | conformance to the project rules named in the brief |
| ~15% | P02801–P02875 (75) | watching it run — headless Playwright on port 4106 |
| ~10% | P02876–P02925 (50) | screenshots actually opened and looked at (1280×800 and A35 360×780 dpr3) |
| ~10% | P02926–P02975 (50) | tracing one change across panels |
| ~5% | P02976–P03000 (25) | judgment — a chef, wet hands, twenty seconds |

Every file in the territory has at least one phase; the three files named in the scope
(`app.js`, `index.html`, `style.css`) have far more than ten each.

Environment for every runtime row: dev server on **port 4106** from the T6 worktree, signed in
ONCE per run as the diag kitchen user via `scripts/sweep/login.mjs` (`loginAs`), restaurant
**French House** (`french-house`, 30 tables, auto-print KOT on). Aangan was never written to.

Result key: ✅ pass · ❌ fault found (fixed in this PR unless the note says otherwise) · ⏭ skipped, with the reason written in the note.

The 60s backstop is correctly skipped. Note: this dev database is shared with five other sweep terminals, so breadcrumbs never stop; a quiet restaurant would see almost nothing · **re-run 2026-08-22:** 135s hidden · 2 whole-board reads, 0 one-table reads, 0 breadcrumbs · budget 6 (auto-print ON — the printer keeps a heartbeat, deliberately) · **EXPECTATION NARROWED 2026-09-03 (sweep #8 T9).** "No whole-board read while hidden" is now true only for an ordinary DISPLAY. A screen that is the PRINTER keeps its 60s backstop while hidden on purpose (owner, 2026-08-17: a covered window stopped hearing about orders at all), and `keepAlive: () => !!state.autoPrintKot` keeps its socket too. Measured this run on a restaurant with auto-print ON: 0 reads in 9s, which the 60s interval explains rather than the rule. The honest check is the CODE shape — `if (!document.hidden || state.autoPrintKot)` — asserted at `P62707`/`P62923`. |

# SECOND PASS — re-run 2026-08-19, against `main`, not this branch

The first pass verified about 300 of these 500 rows by reading the code and saying so. That cannot
catch a regression and nobody else can re-run it. This pass turned every row into an **assertion
that prints a value and can go red**, and proved it by pointing the suite at a deliberately broken
copy of the panel: **10 of 11 planted regressions were caught**.

It also ran against **current `main`**, not this branch. That mattered: by then PR #1007 had merged
and five further commits had edited the kitchen panel — including the print-queue rework (mig 335),
which rewrote the printing path inside this territory. All seven of the first pass's fixes survived
it, and the panel's contract with all nine shared files it loads is intact.

**Result: 500 phases · 489 pass · 0 fail · 11 skip (each with a written reason).**

## What the second pass found that the first pass missed

| what | how it slipped through the first time |
|---|---|
| After a refused ✓, the button never came back **on a ticket with more than one cooking dish** | the first fix restored the DATA and left the SCREEN wrong. A single-dish ticket hides it — finishing it moves the card to Ready, and that rebuild re-stamps `__kdsHtml`. Nothing rebuilds a card that never changed lane. |
| The 👑 VIP mark measured 4.23:1 | the first pass sampled every word on a ticket but not the badges. |
| Every top-bar button was 37–43px above phone width | the first pass measured `.tick`, `.big`, `.reprint` and the 86 drawer — and never the bar one row higher. |
| `--panel-2` and `--fg` read but never declared | arrived with the print-queue merge after the first pass; no fallback, so the new hamburger and the printer sheet's status box had no surface at all. |

## And what the harness found wrong with *itself*

Roughly twenty of these rows were passing or failing for the wrong reason. Worth recording, because
they are the shapes a source-reading check gets wrong:

- **Comments.** 56% of `app.js` is prose (esbuild: 124,881 chars of file, 54,467 of code). A plain
  regex matched sentences DESCRIBING the behaviour — including sentences describing behaviour that
  had been deleted. Every structural row now runs against a comment-blanked copy that keeps offsets.
- **`color(srgb 0.97 …)`.** Chrome reports a `color-mix()` result in 0–1, not 0–255. Read as 0–255 a
  pale background looks almost black and invents a contrast failure. The repo's own
  `verify-skin-ink.mjs` already carries this guard; this suite did not.
- **Fixed waits.** `/api/kitchen/board` was measured at **42 seconds** on the shared dev database. A
  stopwatch measured an empty board and reported an empty kitchen. Rows now wait for the restaurant
  name — the thing `loadImpl` writes only after a read actually lands.
- **Counts and positions.** "exactly two timers", "exactly three back layers", "exactly four sheet
  rows", "the parcel must not be last" — every one of them went red the moment another terminal
  legitimately added something, or the board's newest ticket happened to be the parcel. All are now
  properties: *no timer touches the network faster than 60s*, *every layer is unregistered again*,
  *the rendered order is sorted against `created_at`*.
- **A shared database.** Hidden-tab read counts ranged 1 → 43 in the same window depending on what
  the other terminals were doing. That row now has an explicit budget: one backstop tick a minute
  plus one read per breadcrumb that actually arrived.

## One regression this pass caused, and caught

Widening the top-bar buttons by adding `display: inline-flex` to `.btn` overrode
`.kds-more-btn { display: none }` — same specificity, later in the file — so the phone-only ⋯ button
appeared at **every** width. Nothing about the change looked wrong; only measuring the bar at four
widths found it. The fix reaches 44px with `min-height` alone, and `verify:ready-tile` now refuses a
`display` in that rule.

## Rows whose expectation CHANGED (not failures — the product moved)

| id | was | is |
|----|-----|----|
| P02697 | table names set before `autoPrintNew()` | mig 335 moved printing to a database queue; the same ordering now guards `processPrintJobs()` · **re-run 2026-09-03 (sweep #8 T9):** as expected, mechanically — `npm run verify:kitchen -- --only P02697` |
| P02707 | "the 60s backstop mentions `document.hidden`" — too weak to notice a carve-out | the exact shape, including mig 335's deliberate `\|\| state.autoPrintKot` so an unattended wall display still prints · **re-run 2026-09-03 (sweep #8 T9):** as expected, mechanically — `npm run verify:kitchen -- --only P02707` |
| P02856 | "zero whole-board reads while hidden" | a budget: one backstop tick a minute (only when auto-print is on) plus one read per breadcrumb |
| P02946 / P02955 | the atomic claim and the print log live in the route | both moved to `lib/printQueue.ts`; the contract check follows the code |

## Still open

- **H1** — a QR order placed without joining the session reaches the pass in 2.0s and **rings
  nothing**: it arrives `preparing` with no member id, which the panel reads as a waiter's order.
  Needs a channel column on `orders`; outside this territory.
- **`style.css` is MIXED** (425 CRLF + 61 LF) — it arrived that way with the print-queue merge. Left
  alone on purpose: normalising it would bury every real change under a whole-file diff.
- **The connection pill is 89×25** — the only control on the bar still under 44px, and it belongs to
  `public/panels/connbadge.js`, not here.

# THIRD PASS — sweep #7, 2026-08-22/23, against `origin/main` b64951ad

**Every one of the 500 rows above was re-executed and its result updated in place.** No row was
renumbered and no id was reused. Where a row's expectation had legitimately MOVED — the product
changed under it — the expectation was rewritten and the id kept; those are listed below.

Environment: a dev server on **port 4206** from this terminal's own worktree (`lsof` confirmed the
listening process's cwd is `wt-s7-t6` before anything was trusted), signed in ONCE per role via
`scripts/sweep/login.mjs`, restaurant **French House**. Aangan was never written to. Every row this
run created was retired again the way the product itself retires one — cancel + archive + close the
session + dismiss the queued ticket (mig 190 refuses a hard delete of anything carrying a KOT).

**Result: 500 re-run rows · 0 regressions · 500 new checks · 3 problems found, all three fixed in this branch.**

| P02600 · P02641 | "…then `freshLoad()`" | this run's own item 2: the same fresh read, now through `refreshQuietly()` so a failed read cannot become a row in the owner's Everything Log. Behaviour unchanged. |

The GUARD it asserts — never un-serve a dish — is unchanged, and the row now matches the guard rather than the whole line. · **re-run 2026-09-03 (sweep #8 T9):** as expected, mechanically — `npm run verify:kitchen -- --only P02638` |
| P02894 | "both worded buttons are icon-only and still 44px on a phone" | only 🚫 Sold out stays on the bar (word hidden, 44px). ▦ Board layout MOVES into the ⋯ menu, where the word-hiding rule deliberately stops applying, so it gets its word back and has no height on the bar at all. # FOURTH PASS — 500 NEW phases, 2026-08-29/30 · `P32101`–`P32600`

Run after the owner said *"can do any design you want, you can merge and make it live and plan 500
phases test again and run again … Make sure everything that you have build is perfectly running"*.

Everything built for this screen since the third pass is under test here: the four improvements he
picked (one note once · the sold-out switch · the columns in time order · a guest's order ringing),
the board redesign, and the three problems the third pass fixed — re-asserted so they cannot rot.

Environment: a dev server on **port 4251** from this session's own worktree (`lsof` confirmed the
listening process's cwd before anything was trusted — 4207 turned out to belong to another session,
which is exactly why the check exists). Restaurant **French House**; **Aangan never written to**.
Every row created was retired the way the product retires one. The final section re-runs the same
assertions against **https://3-d-backup.vercel.app**, so "it works" and "it is live" are two answers.

**Result: 500 phases · 496 pass · 0 fail · 4 skip.**

**One fault found, and it was in this run's own work:** P32243 caught the count on the "Sold out
only" switch going stale the moment a cook marked a dish off — the drawer deliberately does not
rebuild on a toggle (it would lose the search caret and orphan the UNDO), so the number sat at 0
while dishes went off. Fixed by patching that one number in place, and the list is still left
alone so the row the UNDO bar is talking about stays on screen.

edited here, not in `app/manager/`. Terminal 7 has the rest of the same file.

the owner's own window), one login per role for the whole run, French House. Aangan was never

## 2. Watching it run — every tab, every view, every Settings section — P59966–P60062

Headless Chromium against a dev server on port 4306 from this worktree, signed in ONCE as the diag manager (`scripts/sweep/login.mjs`), restaurant French House. Every assertion is on the RENDERED page — `innerText`, `offsetParent`, a measured box — never on the source.

## 5. What this run FOUND — P60205–P60216

One row per fault, with how it was proved. Eight fixed in this PR, one reported and not fixed with the reason, and one row recording that my own detectors were wrong four times before any red was believed.

| id | check | how to verify | result | note |
|----|-------|---------------|--------|------|
| P60205 | Settings > Printing asked Rename and Unlink with the BROWSER's own dialog | read both call sites; every other ask in this file uses promptDialog/confirmDialog | ❌ | FIXED item 1. prompt() answers null and confirm() answers false with nothing shown on a kiosk/webview/'no more dialogs' Chrome, and both sites return on a falsy answer — the tap vanished |
| P60206 | …and verify:panel-dialogs never looked at editor/app.js at all | read the guard: readdirSync(public/panels) is the TOP LEVEL, plus editor/inventory.js by name | ❌ | FIXED item 1. The largest panel file in the repo was outside the guard that exists for exactly this |
| P60207 | billsCapped() reads fields the bills read never returns | compare its reads (today, previous) with the one assignment (rows, parcels, reach, at) | ❌ | FIXED item 2. Always false, so the Previous-bills search never asked the server and the route's whole type=any branch was unreachable |
| P60208 | …proved by forcing a genuinely capped window | set state.billsRec.rows to 500 rows in the running panel and call it | ❌ | FIXED item 2. Returned false at a full 500. After the fix: true at 500, false at 499, and rows+parcels count together |
| P60209 | restoreBill() throws on the path where every write SUCCEEDED | ESLint no-undef over the panel, then driven in the running panel with api() stubbed | ❌ | FIXED item 3. '_wq is not defined'. The PATCH goes out, the bill IS restored, zero toasts. The failure branch toasts fine, so it spoke up when wrong and went silent when right |
| P60210 | openDishEditModal()'s save has the identical fault | ESLint no-undef; the same const-in-a-block shape | ❌ | FIXED item 4 (outside my line range, disclosed). Editing a guest's allergy or note from an opened bill threw every time, after the writes had landed |
| P60211 | printing one bill relabels EVERY print button on the page as Reprint | read markBillPrintedLocally: querySelectorAll('[data-bill-print-btn]') with no scope | ❌ | FIXED item 5. Bills > Live draws one per running table, so T5's print renamed T9's untouched button |
| P60212 | the Undo after deleting a dish reports the DELETE's queue flag, not the restore's | read undoRec: okToast(_wq, ...) where _wq is the DELETE's answer | ❌ | FIXED item 6. Delete online, go offline, press Undo -> a plain 'Restored' over a write still sitting in the queue |
| P60213 | 'My profile & pay' is broken on EVERY staff panel and denies the person is staff | ESLint no-undef on maint.js, then driven: call LFH_PROFILE_GET() and open LFH_ME | ❌ | FIXED item 7 (outside my territory, disclosed). 'deadline is not defined' from two helpers outside the IIFE that declares it. Live since 2026-08-30 (462442a2). available() answered false while the server said profileModule:true; opening it read 'You are not signed in as a staff member'. Screenshot: shots/T6/myprofile.png |
| P60214 | five declarations in the top half are dead, and their comments describe behaviour the panel no longer has | count every mention of each UPPER_CASE constant in stripped code | ❌ | FIXED item 8. REASONS_DELETE, REASONS_CLOSE, STATUS_META, STATUS_RANK and a data-ch attribute. STATUS_META's comment carried a real cross-panel rule about the word 'Preparing' attached to a map nothing reads |
| P60215 | the table NUMBER disappears from every floor tile at phone width | measure .ft-num's rendered width per tile at 1440 / 1024 / 820 / 600 / 430 / 390 / 360px | ❌ | NOT FIXED — public/panels/editor/style.css is outside this terminal's territory, and it is layout CSS that needs looking at across every width. Visible down to a 52px tile (430px viewport); at 47px (390px) and 42px (360px) it is 0px wide on 31 of 31 tiles. innerText still reads '1\|4\|Free', so no text-based check sees it. The seat count (16px, never shrinks) wins the row while .ft-num has flex 0 1 auto + min-width:0 + overflow:hidden. Screenshots: shots/T6/phone-390.png, shots/T6/phone-tile.png. Reported as item 12 |
| P60216 | my own detectors were wrong four times before any red was believed | open every failing check by hand or by eye before filing it | ✅ | Recorded deliberately. (1) the floor-tile selector was guessed and found 0 of 31 tiles; (2) an empty state's sentence was cut at the first tag; (3) contrast walked past gradients; (4) contrast read rgba(...,0.12) as solid. Every one would have been an invented fault. Four of five reds in the first pass were mine, not the product's |
